### 2026-07-29 05:22:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Oil Inventories Unexpectedly Rise — US crude oil inventories unexpectedly increased by 3.3 million barrels in the week ending July 24, defying market expectations for a 2.5 million-barrel draw. The build followed a 2.6 million-barrel increase in the previous week. Despite the recent gains, commercial crude inventories excluding the Strategic Petroleum Reserve (SPR) have declined by more than 54 million barrels over the past 15 weeks and remain about 3 million barrels lower year to date. Another 3.7 million barrels were withdrawn from the SPR during the week, reducing holdings to 307.7 million barrels, just above the widely accepted operational minimum range of 250-300 million barrels. At the Cushing, Oklahoma hub, inventories fell by 273,000 barrels after a 737,000-barrel decline the previous week. US crude production edged down to 13.80 million bpd from 13.86 million bpd. Meanwhile, gasoline inventories rose by 918,000 barrels, while distillate stockpiles fell by 125,000 barrels.

Ibovespa Gains as BCB Rate Cut Bets Grow — The Ibovespa rose 0.7% to close at 176,565 on Tuesday after Brazil's mid-July inflation report came in below expectations. Annual inflation slowed to 4.52%, below forecasts of 4.67% and down from 4.8% in June. The softer reading, together with June's lower-than-expected inflation data, reinforced expectations of a 25-basis-point Selic rate cut next week. Financials led the gains, with Banco do Brasil up 1.6% and B3 adding 1.8%. Bradesco (+0.3%) and Santander (+2.2%) are due to report earnings tomorrow. Utilities also rose, with Axia rising 1.9%. Ambev gained 1.5% ahead of its earnings release on Thursday. WEG extended gains, rising 0.6% after Goldman Sachs upgraded the stock to Neutral from Sell, citing more balanced market expectations. In contrast, Vivo (-6.3%) and TIM (-5.8%) tumbled after reporting results. TIM's mobile service revenue growth slowed to its weakest pace since 2021, while Vivo saw an 8% increase in depreciation and amortization expenses.

US Stocks Mixed as Chip Selloff Deepens — US equity indices closed mixed on Tuesday as chipmakers extended their selloff, while strength in software and defensive sectors supported the broader market. The S&P 500 gained 0.2%, the Nasdaq 100 fell 1%, and the Dow rose 537 points. Concerns over slowing AI infrastructure spending and the circular nature of data center investment weighed on compute and memory chipmakers. Micron slumped 8.8%, AMD lost 8.1%, Intel fell 5.9%, and Sandisk tumbled 14.2%. The sector also came under pressure after the strong IPO of China's CXMT in Shanghai boosted optimism over rising competition. Meanwhile, software and non-tech stocks outperformed, with Microsoft up 1.1% and Meta closing little changed ahead of earnings on Wednesday. Lower oil prices and renewed US signals favoring diplomacy with Iran eased credit costs and supported the broader market. Berkshire Hathaway gained 3.1%, while Visa rose 1.1% ahead of its earnings release. Coca-Cola jumped 7.1% after raising its full-year outlook.

FTSE MIB Closes Lower — The FTSE MIB fell 0.7% to close at 51,698 on Tuesday as the war in the Middle East hampered Saipem's corporate results, while growing skepticism on AI overspending dented equity demand across the globe. Saipem tumbled 8.8% after lowering its core earnings forecast, citing higher costs and logistical disruptions linked to the Gulf conflict. Other energy stocks also declined, with Tenaris and Eni losing more than 1.5%. Semiconductor stocks remained under pressure as investors questioned elevated valuations amid concerns over AI investments ahead of earnings reports from major tech firms. Prysmian dropped 4.3%, while STMicroelectronics lost 3.1%. Banking stocks underperformed, led by BMPS (-1.9%) and Banco BPM (-1.8%), as reports of a potential merger between the two lenders raised uncertainty over Intesa Sanpaolo's (-0.4%) takeover bid. On the upside, Campari (3.4%), Amplifon (3%), and Ferrari (2.9%) extended gains from the previous session.

### 2026-07-28 23:53:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

US Dollar Eases from 15-Month High — The dollar index softened to 101.3 on Tuesday after testing a 15-month high of 101.6 in the previous session as softer energy prices limited the outlook of a rate hike by the Federal Reserve this year. US officials stressed that a diplomatic resolution may still be achieved with Iran in their war, driving fuel prices to ease off their local peaks and trimming the risk of a rebound in energy inflation. Still, fuel prices remained elevated in a historical level as exports from the Middle East are still low. A portion of FOMC members had recently signaled that higher inflation and evidence of a robust labor market could warrant higher rates. The Federal Reserve is expected to hold its policy rates unchanged tomorrow, but rate futures continued reflect the consensus of a hike this year. The greenback held recent gains against the euro and the yen, although political volatility in the UK maintained the sterling's recent appreciation.

【Zhongji Xuchuang: 1.6T ASP far above market rumours; no destructive price competition】Zhongji Xuchuang told investors its 1.6T ASP is well above market rumours and the industry is not engaging in cutthroat price competition. Overall demand is very strong, raw materials are scarce and 1.6T deliveries are tight; only a few vendors can deliver 1.6T at scale. The company says its pricing is competitive and can sustain current gross margins. Nearly all customer orders already cover full-year 2026, with some orders extending into 2027 and delivery schedules broken down monthly. For 2027 the company sees clear, high-certainty demand and rapid growth for 800G, 1.6T, 2.4T and NPO products; several key customers have issued sizeable new-product guidance for 2028. Zhongji Xuchuang expects downstream key customers’ capex to remain robust and customer investment in the sector to continue rising.

### 2026-07-28 23:34:25  #MKT News #Commodities #Precious Metals #XAUUSD #Impact mixed

【Reuters survey: analysts lower gold forecasts for first time since end-2023; central bank buying seen cushioning decline】A Reuters survey of 29 analysts and traders over the past three weeks shows forecasters trimmed gold price forecasts for the first time since end-2023 after a sharp retreat from January’s record high. The median 2026 forecast is $4,509/oz, down from $4,916 three months earlier and marking the first quarterly downgrade in 11 quarters. The 2027 forecast averaged $4,610/oz, versus $5,100 in the prior survey. Most respondents still expect central bank buying and fiscal-sustainability concerns to provide support. Gold hit a record $5,595/oz in January but plunged in Q2 as the Iran war intensified energy-driven inflation and lifted rate-hike expectations, producing the worst quarter since 2013; spot gold is down about 22% since the conflict began.

【受颱風“紅霞”影響的廣東使用者已全部恢復供電】28日從南方電網瞭解到，截至18時，受今年第12號颱風“紅霞”影響的廣東使用者已全部恢復供電。26日凌晨，颱風“紅霞”的中心在廣東省惠州市惠東縣平海鎮登陸，登陸時中心附近最大風力14級（45米/秒）。颱風登陸時，部分電力設施遭颱風損毀，不少居民家中出現斷電情況。隨後，電力部門組織搶險力量赴臺風登陸點開展搶修工作。據電力部門介紹，在臺風襲擊廣東期間，惠州、汕尾等地50餘萬戶使用者用電受到影響。廣東電網公司在其供電營業區組建了200餘支搶險隊伍，奮戰60餘小時，目前已全部恢復供電。深圳供電局派出搶險人員900餘人次，奔赴受颱風影響較嚴重的深汕特別合作區開展搶修工作，順利恢復當地供電。（新華社）

【伊朗軍方警告不要接受美國使用伊資產進行“賠償”】據伊朗方面28日訊息，伊朗武裝部隊哈塔姆安比亞中央總部發言人說，接受美國使用伊朗資產進行所謂賠償的任何國家或公司，其所屬船隻將不被允許透過霍爾木茲海峽。發言人說，美國總統特朗普此前宣佈，將利用伊朗被凍結資產，賠償伊美衝突期間受損船隻。伊方認為，這些船隻受損是由於美軍制造地區不安全域性勢，以及“違規駛入霍爾木茲海峽南部非法且不安全的航道”所致。他警告稱，即日起，任何支援特朗普上述提議和使用伊朗被凍結資產的國家或公司所屬船隻將被禁止透過霍爾木茲海峽。

### 2026-07-28 23:02:03  #公司 #國際

【全球最大單批次綠氨出口啟航 中國綠色燃料獲國際認可】7月28日訊息，國家電投集團電投綠能3750噸“氫洲·綠氨”今天從連雲港離岸起航發往韓國，此次出海創下全球最大單批次綠氨出口紀錄，也是中國綠色燃料首次憑藉全生命週期碳減排貢獻，獲得國際市場綠色價值認可。本次出口的綠氨產品，全部產自國家電投集團“氫洲·大安綠氨”示範專案。該專案作為國家清潔低碳氫能示範工程、戰略性新興產業“百大工程”，2025年7月建成投產，配套新能源裝機80萬千瓦，可年產綠氫3.2萬噸、綠氨18萬噸，一舉斬獲全球最大單體綠氨裝置等五項“全球之最”。“氫洲·大安綠氨”示範專案2025年10月獲得全球首張“非生物來源可再生燃料氨（RFNBO Ammonia）”認證。本批次出口綠氨首次以“全生命週期碳減排貢獻”獲得國際市場綠色價值認可。經測算，3750噸綠氨相較國內傳統煤制合成氨，全生命週期可減排二氧化碳超萬噸，等效於約100萬棵樹木一年的固碳量。當前，國際海事組織碳強度指標趨嚴、歐盟碳排放交易體系全面覆蓋航運領域，全球可溯源、可核查的綠色燃料市場需求持續攀升。本次規模化出口，標誌著中國綠氨產品正式深度嵌入全球綠色燃料供應鏈。（新浪科技）

### 2026-07-28 23:02:01  #央行 #美聯儲

【美國7月消費者信心下降 民眾對營商環境和就業市場看法惡化】美國7月消費者信心下降，民眾對當前商業環境和勞動力市場的看法惡化。世界大型企業聯合會週二公佈的資料顯示，消費者信心指數下降1.4點至90.8，前一個月資料則獲向上修正。經濟學家預估中值為92.4。衡量當前狀況的指標降至2021年以來最低，而反映對未來六個月預期的指標持平。汽油和食品價格高企可能對美國家庭造成壓力，進一步凸顯市場對通脹持續和生活成本上升的廣泛擔憂。與此同時，在春季表現強勁後，招聘活動已出現放緩跡象。世界大型企業聯合會的調查顯示，7月消費者對就業市場的看法轉弱。認為職位充足的受訪者比例降至24.6%，認為工作難找的比例也小幅下降。這兩個比例之間的差值是經濟學家密切關注的指標，7月收窄至2021年以來最小，表明求職者面臨的勞動力市場環境較為艱難。本次調查覆蓋7月1日至22日。其間，美國加油站汽油價格先降至3月以來最低，隨後因美伊衝突再度升級、油價上漲而反彈。美國上週末暫停空襲後，市場情緒趨穩，油價回落。

---

### 2026-07-28 22:45:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Dallas Fed Services Index #Importance 1

Dallas Fed Business Activity Rises Most in 11 Months — The general business activity index for the service sector published by the Federal Reserve Bank of Dallas rose to 6.6 in July of 2026 from 2.9 in the previous month, marking the sharpest pace of growth in 11 months. The result was aligned with other similar surveys in the period as exports of energy in the Middle East were momentarily restored, lowering energy prices and operating costs to improve margin prospects in the sector. Revenue growth eased slightly (9.8 vs 9.5 in June), although hours worked rose faster (4.9 vs 3.6). Meanwhile, input prices slowed (33.5 vs 36.5) while selling charges rose sharply (14.2 vs 7.7). Looking ahead, the general business conditions for companies rose to 21 from 17.1.

【MOFCOM legal department head on WTO panel report in China v Turkey over Turkish restrictions on electric vehicles (DS629)】Geneva, July 28 — The WTO published the panel report in China v Turkey (DS629). MOFCOM’s treaty and legal department said the panel upheld China’s claims and found Turkey’s measures on electric and other vehicles in breach of WTO rules. China welcomed the ruling and urged Turkey to respect the panel report and promptly correct the measures.

### 2026-07-28 22:35:36  #焦點 #國際

【商務部條約法律司負責人就世貿組織釋出我訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施世貿爭端案專家組報告答記者問】
有記者問：日內瓦時間7月28日，世貿組織公佈了中國訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施世貿爭端案專家組報告。請問中方對此有何評論？ 

答：我們注意到，世貿組織散發了中國訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施案（案件編號：DS629）專家組報告。專家組支援中方主張，裁定土方涉案措施違反世貿規則。中方對此裁決表示歡迎。

2023年以來，土方對中國出口的電動汽車、混動汽車等車輛，先後採取附加關稅、進口許可證等限制措施，違反約束關稅、國民待遇、最惠國待遇等多邊貿易規則，嚴重損害中方正當貿易利益和中土經貿關係。中方敦促土方尊重世貿組織專家組裁決，採取切實行動，儘快糾正違規措施。

同時，我想指出，中方堅定奉行互利共贏的開放戰略，堅定維護以世貿組織為核心的多邊貿易體制。中方願在符合多邊貿易規則的基礎上，與各方不斷提升在新能源汽車等領域的貿易投資合作質量和水平，實現共同發展。（商務部）

2026上海合作組織媒體智庫峰會專家論壇27日在吉爾吉斯斯坦首都比什凱克舉行。與會專家學者表示，上合組織的成就證明，建立在相互理解和互利共贏基礎上的多邊合作，不僅有利於促進和平與安定，也有利於促進可持續發展。數智時代面臨前所未有的機遇和挑戰，各國需要攜手努力，共同塑造一個安全、有韌性、包容且有利於可持續發展的國際資訊環境。
“今日俄羅斯”國際媒體集團國際合作局局長瓦西里·普什科夫說，世界正經歷動盪變革，迫切需要智庫專家與媒體攜手合作，在加強相互理解、互信與睦鄰友好等方面發揮重要作用，為開展坦誠而有針對性的對話打造平臺。
白俄羅斯國立大學國際研究中心主任尼基塔·別連琴科說，隨著人工智慧技術快速發展，傳統資訊保安理念已難以應對新的挑戰，各國應重視資訊環境韌性和資訊自主能力，需要推動建立聯合監測預警機制，深化人工智慧領域研究與媒體、智庫交流，共同維護數智時代的資訊保安。
埃及金字塔政治和戰略研究中心副主任艾哈邁德·坎迪勒說，“上海精神”為世界提供了一條既區別於強權支配也不同於失序混亂的發展路徑，各國可以依託協商實現互利共贏。
寮國社會與經濟科學院社會經濟政策研究所所長吳沙萬·天特翁薩說，寮國致力於與上合組織夥伴密切合作，加強資訊共享，“更好地保護我們的數字環境和發展成果”。
尼泊爾中國研究中心秘書長烏彭德拉·高塔姆說，真誠協作創造的價值，遠大於各國獨自耕耘的總和。上合組織越來越務實、包容、以人為本，將進一步助力構建人類命運共同體。
新華社研究院院長劉剛說，25年來，上合組織在捍衛以聯合國為核心的國際體系和以國際法為基礎的國際秩序中發揮了重要作用。當前，單邊主義、保護主義持續衝擊全球秩序，地緣博弈加劇考驗區域協作效能，上合組織迎來提質升級的關鍵視窗期，需要加強合作，架起智庫溝通的橋樑。
與會專家學者認為，上合組織堅持求同存異、互利共贏、開放包容、公平正義、務實高效的原則，在確保地區安全穩定、推動地區繁榮、促進人文交流、維護國際公平正義、提升組織行動能力等方面取得了開創性成果，將在維護和平、促進發展、推動全球治理體系變革等方面發揮重要作用。
這次專家論壇由新華通訊社、“今日俄羅斯”國際媒體集團共同舉辦。來自中國、俄羅斯、吉爾吉斯斯坦、哈薩克、巴基斯坦、印度、斯里蘭卡、阿聯酋、寮國等十餘個國家的相關專家學者與會。（小故事）

【Recycled copper market tightens as premium scrap snapped up on arrival】Traders in Taizhou, Zhejiang report sustained copper price gains and acute demand for high‑purity scrap; premium scrap consignments are being bought the moment they arrive. Dealers are dismantling motors, cables and radiator units and sorting metal streams to meet downstream appetite. Demand expansion from EVs, AI and other industrial users, combined with constrained growth in primary copper smelting capacity, is broadening demand for recycled copper and creating opportunities in China’s scrap recovery market. Chinese recycled-copper products are also attracting overseas buyers on the back of lower carbon intensity, lower energy use, stable quality and traceable carbon footprints.

### 2026-07-28 22:16:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Richmond Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 1

US Fifth District Manufacturing Edges Up in July — The Federal Reserve's Fifth District manufacturing index edged up to 5 in July 2026 from 4 in June, remaining well below market expectations of 9. Manufacturing activity was broadly stable during the month, as stronger shipments (8 vs. 4) and a rebound in employment (2 vs. -1) offset slower growth in new orders (5 vs. 8). Local business conditions improved sharply, with the index rising to 10 from -1. On the price front, cost pressures eased, as the growth rate of prices paid slowed to 6.08% from 6.99%, while prices received moderated to 3.96% from 4.57%. Looking ahead, firms expect inflationary pressures to moderate over the next 12 months. However, optimism softened, with expectations for local business conditions (19 vs. 22), shipments (33 vs. 36), and new orders (31 vs. 33) all declining, though remaining firmly in positive territory.

Richmond Fed Services Index Remains Flat in July — The Richmond Fed Fifth District Service Sector Survey was broadly unchanged in July 2026, marking a second consecutive month of flat activity. The revenues index slipped to -3 from 0, while the demand index edged up to 5 from 4. Current local business conditions improved slightly to -5 from -7, although expectations weakened, with the future business conditions index falling to 9 from 16. Employment growth moderated, as the current employment index declined to 4 from 8, while the forward-looking employment gauge was little changed at 23, pointing to continued hiring plans. Wage growth remained firm, with both current and expected wage indexes staying in positive territory. Price pressures eased slightly, as the growth rate of prices paid slowed, while prices received rose marginally. Firms expect both measures to moderate further over the next 12 months.

【AI行業規模激增 Anthropic營收超越星巴克、麥當勞】人工智慧相關收入增速迅猛，體量已開始碾壓一眾全球頂級消費品牌。當投資者直觀看到AI投入的兌現能力，數萬億美元級的AI基建開支預期也就更容易被理解。 根據AI投研平臺Funda的資料，OpenAI與Anthropic兩家公司年化營收合計有望達到1200億美元。 其中Anthropic憑藉企業級客戶優勢貢獻約60%份額（OpenAI正在全力縮小這一差距）。按照該營收規模，兩家企業均可躋身《財富》美國500強營收百強榜單。 Anthropic預估年化營收約710億美元，超過星巴克（372億美元）與麥當勞（269億美元）兩家營收總和； 該數值幾乎等同於星巴克+麥當勞再加塔可貝爾、肯德基母公司百勝餐飲三者營收之和。

【Consumer confidence slips again — is US consumption resilience faltering?】The Conference Board's July consumer confidence index fell to 90.8, below consensus 92.3 and June's 91.2. The decline was modest and does not signal a sudden consumer collapse, but the index's prolonged low level indicates high inflation, higher rates and job-market uncertainty continue to weigh on households. In June, the current-business-and-employment assessment dropped to 116.4 and the share saying jobs are hard to find rose to the highest since January 2021; the expectations subindex recovered to 74.4 but remains below the 80 recession-warning threshold. Market takeaway: the print is mildly dollar- and yield-negative and slightly supportive of easing expectations, but the miss is limited — decisive policy implications should wait for upcoming GDP, PCE and jobs data.

Gold prices fell as a stronger US dollar and mounting uncertainty ahead of Wednesday's Federal Reserve policy decision weighed on the metal. Agnico Eagle, Barrick, and WPM each lost nearly 3%. Imperial Oil and Cenovus fell nearly 1%, while Canadian Natural lost more than 0.5%. In contrast, technology stocks outperformed after Celestica (+1%). Shopify gained 2% and Constellation Software rose 2.5%. TFI International (+7%) also beat forecasts. ### 2026-07-28 21:59:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Cotton Falls from One-Week High — Cotton prices fell below 81 cents per pound after reaching a one-week high on July 23rd, as easing weather concerns dampened expectations of tighter supplies. In India, a revival in monsoon rains accelerated cotton planting, after the having arrived three days late. Following its fifth driest June on record, the country's rainfall deficit narrowed to 16% from nearly 40% at the end of June, as widespread rains in recent weeks helped provide rain-fed irrigation to farmland's, easing fears of a supply shortfall. Meanwhile, lower oil prices weighed on demand, reducing cotton's competitiveness against polyester fibers. However, India's cotton-sown area was still down 4% year-on-year at 9.87 million hectares, reflecting slower planting across several major producing states. In Brazil, growers are expected to reduce cotton planted area by 4% in the 2025/26 season despite record exports in 2024/25, as weaker prices discouraged acreage expansion.

【我國將建設國有文物資源資料登記管理平臺】記者7月28日從國家文物局舉行的新聞釋出會上獲悉，國家文物局將按照新出臺的《國有文物資源資料管理辦法》規定，建設國有文物資源資料登記管理平臺，編制登記管理制度，統籌佈局國家及區域儲存中心，確保政策落地見效，充分釋放國有文物資源資料要素價值。近年來，社會各界對文物資料的需求日益增長。國家文物局科技教育司司長羅靜介紹，《國有文物資源資料管理辦法》明晰了國有文物資源資料權益主體，明確了國有文物資源資料責任主體，構建了資料採集、加工、儲存、傳輸、登記、服務全生命週期管理制度，對實現文物資料權益清晰、開放有序、利用有效、安全可控的科學管理目標，指導文物行業開展文物數字化實踐具有重要意義。辦法還強調國有文物管理機構應遵循公正、公平、便民的原則，面向社會提供主動公開、授權使用等服務，推動文物保護與數字化發展成果更多更公平惠及廣大群眾。（新華社）

Ibovespa Jumps on Soft Mid-Month Inflation — The Ibovespa rose 1% to trade above 177,000 on Tuesday after Brazil's mid-July inflation report came in below expectations. The softer reading, together with June's lower-than-expected inflation data, reinforced expectations of a 25-basis-point Selic rate cut next week. Financials posted gains, with Itaú, Itaúsa, and Banco do Brasil up around 1.5%, while Bradesco gained nearly 2%. Bradesco and Santander (+2%) are due to report earnings on Wednesday. Utilities also advanced, with Axia rising nearly 3% and Sabesp adding almost 1.5%. Ambev gained about 2% ahead of its earnings release on Thursday. WEG extended recent gains, rising 1% after Goldman Sachs upgraded the stock to Neutral from Sell, citing more balanced market expectations. The company secured a contract from ENGIE Brasil to supply generating units for the R$1.2 billion Jaguara hydroelectric plant expansion project.

### 2026-07-28 21:41:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Falls Further — Brent crude oil fell to around $86 per barrel on Tuesday, touching its lowest level in more than a week, as renewed diplomatic efforts over the Strait of Hormuz improved the outlook for Middle East oil supplies. Iran’s Foreign Minister Abbas Araghchi held separate talks with his Saudi and Omani counterparts, focusing on restoring security in the strategic waterway after disruptions caused by the US-Iran conflict. Oman has proposed a joint regional mechanism to manage the strait, allowing Iran to collect voluntary transit fees, a plan that has reportedly gained support from Gulf states. Also, President Donald Trump said Washington was holding “good talks” with Tehran, although he warned military strikes could resume if diplomacy fails, while Iran also threatened retaliation. Despite the diplomatic progress, regional risks remain elevated after Saudi Arabia intercepted drones targeting oil facilities and Yemen’s Houthi rebels claimed an attack on the kingdom’s East-West pipeline.

Chip Stocks Extend Selloff — US equity indices were mixed on Tuesday as chip producers extended their selloff, contrasting with relief on other sectors. The S&P 500 was flat, while the Nasdaq 100 dropped 1% and the Dow gained 300 points. Concerns of slowing spending on AI infrastructure and the circularity of data center investments grappled compute and memory producers, with Micron, Sandisk dropping more than 7%. The sector was also punished by optimism on Chinese competitors following the strong CXMT IPO in Shanghai. Nvidia fell 1.5% and AMD lost around 5%. On the other hand, software-facing and non-tech shares were mostly higher, with Microsoft and Meta inching higher ahead of their earnings tomorrow. Credit costs extended their pullback, tracking lower energy pries as US officials signaled willingness for diplomacy with Iran. JPMorgan was higher to set the pace for the banks, while Visa gained 1% ahead of its earning release after the close. Also, Coca Cola gained 3% after boosting its outlook.

Silver Slides on Stronger Dollar, Fed Rate Uncertainty — Silver prices fell about 2% to around $57 an ounce on Tuesday, pressured by a stronger US dollar and investor caution ahead of Wednesday's Federal Reserve policy decision. The dollar remained near a four-week high as markets priced in more than a one-third chance of a rate hike, an unusually high level of uncertainty this close to a Fed meeting despite President Donald Trump's repeated calls for lower interest rates. Investors also continue to assign roughly an 80% probability to a rate increase in September, reinforcing expectations that US borrowing costs could stay elevated. Meanwhile, optimism over a potential easing of tensions between the US and Iran reduced demand for safe-haven assets. On Monday, Trump said Washington was engaged in "good talks" with Iran and that a deal to resolve the conflict was possible, while warning that military strikes could resume if negotiations break down.

【阿曼提出聯合管理霍爾木茲海峽新機制】一名海灣地區訊息人士28日披露，阿曼向伊朗提出了一項聯合管理霍爾木茲海峽航運的地區機制，通行海峽的船隻可基於自願原則捐款。目前，阿曼和伊朗官方均未證實這一訊息。這名不願公開姓名的訊息人士表示，新機制參考馬六甲海峽的管理辦法，使用方自願向維護航道的相關基金捐款，用於環境保護和搜救工作等。阿曼的最新提議得到了一些地區國家支援。暫不清楚伊朗會如何回應這一提議。伊朗外交部長阿拉格齊27日與阿曼以及沙烏地阿拉伯外交大臣分別通話，討論霍爾木茲海峽事宜。根據伊朗外交部發表的宣告，阿拉格齊在通話中強調“加強合作的必要性”。阿曼外交代表團24日抵達伊朗首都德黑蘭。據悉，阿曼此訪旨在建立霍爾木茲海峽航運管理的適當機制。據伊朗方面26日訊息，伊朗外交部發言人巴加埃表示，伊朗與阿曼就管理霍爾木茲海峽安全航運的共同原則及操作機制進行討論，會談“富有成效並取得一些進展”。他說，阿曼代表團已於25日離開德黑蘭，但雙方技術和政治磋商仍將繼續。國際市場服務機構克普勒公司資料顯示，儘管美國和伊朗暫停相互襲擊，24日至26日，霍爾木茲海峽的每日貨船通行量均為個位數。另據伊朗知情官員訊息，27日凌晨，有6艘關閉導航和定位系統的船隻試圖經霍爾木茲海峽南部一條“非法且不安全”的航道航行，其中1艘發生事故，其餘船隻被“引導”返回波斯灣。（央視新聞）

【招商證券：韓國股市去槓桿加速，A股政策底顯現迎穩市訊號】韓國股市槓桿資金正經歷快速收縮。韓國金融投資協會資料顯示，全市場信用融資餘額自6月24日的38.6萬億韓元歷史峰值，至7月24日已回落至32.67萬億韓元，降幅達15.4%。其中，KOSDAQ板塊收縮更為顯著（降幅22%），快於KOSPI主機板（-13.5%）。在科技股暴跌引發的強制平倉潮中，自6月19日以來累計強平金額達1.1萬億韓元。值得注意的是，槓桿ETF的收縮速度遠超融資餘額，成為市場下跌的高效“加速器”。以SAMSUNG KODEX 2倍做多ETF為例，其規模從6月高點驟降48%至38億元，其機械式的每日再平衡機制形成了無間斷的自我強化迴路。外部槓桿去化雖對A股情緒形成一定傳導，但國內穩市訊號正密集釋放，A股政策底已然顯現。一方面，證監會堅定維護資本市場平穩健康執行；另一方面，北京國管、北京控股等多家央國企及上市公司集中披露增持、回購及提高分紅安排，彰顯發展信心。資金面上，上週寬基ETF大幅淨流入超500億元。往後看，隨著穩市座談會召開，監管有望進一步加大穩市力度。若後續市場超預期下行，穩市措施有望對標去年4月，從資金支援、央企回購、金融機構增持等多方面入手，以穩定資本市場並增強投資者信心。

【甘肅局地啟動洪水防禦Ⅲ級應急響應】記者從甘肅省水利廳獲悉，受降雨和上游來水影響，甘肅省酒泉市肅北蒙古族自治縣境內黨城灣鎮段水位快速上漲，黨河黨城灣水文站7月28日16時流量311立方米每秒，超過洪水橙色預警標準250立方米每秒，接近紅色預警標準350立方米每秒。根據《甘肅省水利廳水旱災害防禦應急預案》規定，甘肅省水利廳決定7月28日17時起，針對酒泉市啟動洪水防禦Ⅲ級應急響應。 甘肅省水利廳、甘肅省氣象局7月28日13時釋出山洪災害氣象風險藍色預警，預計28日13時至29日14時，酒泉市（瓜州縣、肅北縣、阿克塞縣、玉門市、敦煌市）區域性地方降水量可達大到暴雨，可能發生山洪災害。其他地區也可能因局地短歷時強降水引發山洪災害。 甘肅省水利廳提示，相關部門要注意做好實時監測、防汛預警，緊盯河流、水庫水位，加密水情監測，做好涉河涉溝在建工程和沿河作業場所洪水防禦工作，確保人民群眾生命財產安全。（新華社）

### 2026-07-28 21:23:51  #其他

【貸款利率定價有了新“尺子” 全國首單已落地】近日，三家銀行在海南落地了全國首單三筆DR（存款類金融機構間債券回購利率）定價貸款。具體來看，工商銀行海南省分行、招商銀行海口分行、浦發銀行海口分行三家銀行立足海南自貿港跨境經營主體集聚、EF賬戶體系較為成熟、金融開放場景豐富等優勢，主動探索DR基準利率貸款創新應用，在海南自貿港落地全國首單三筆DR基準利率貸款。當前，我國銀行的貸款利率主要是在LPR基礎上加點而來。而本次三家銀行的嘗試被視為一種新的可能性，為深化利率市場化改革提供了可複製、可推廣的海南樣本。“DR屬於交易形成型利率，區別於LPR的報價型利率。”一位業內人士向記者解釋。DR依託海量市場交易生成，單一主體難以干預，市場化程度更高，這也意味著貸款利率定價機制從報價型向交易型轉變。（金融時報）

【藍龍蝦降價近20%】藍龍蝦，原產澳大利亞，國內部分地區已有大規模養殖。廣東珠三角區域是國內藍龍蝦主產區之一，眼下正值消費旺季。在廣東佛山高明區的一處蝦塘，養殖戶告訴央視財經記者，每天都有幾個貿易商過來拿蝦，目前38元到40元一斤。從廣州趕來採購的貿易商告訴記者，今年市場行情向好，隨著養殖成本降低，藍龍蝦的價格下行帶動銷量明顯上漲。各種規格藍龍蝦的價格普遍下降20%左右。記者走訪發現，隨著育苗技術實現突破，蝦苗成本較往年明顯下降，藍龍蝦養殖經濟效益持續提升，不少養殖戶紛紛擴大規模。資料顯示，2024年廣東佛山高明區養殖藍龍蝦達2100多畝，2026年躍升至6000畝，預計今年產值接近2億元。（央視財經）

US Home Price Growth Accelerates in May — The S&P Cotality Case-Shiller 20-City Home Price Index rose 1.6% year-over-year in May 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 1.2% increase in April and exceeding market expectations of 1.3%. The annual gain was the strongest since August 2025. However, after adjusting for inflation, home prices declined for a 12th consecutive month, as May's 4.2% inflation rate outpaced nominal home price growth by 2.6 percentage points. For a third straight month, Chicago recorded the strongest annual price increase among the 20 cities, up 6.9%, followed by New York (4.2%) and Cleveland (3.1%). Meanwhile, Las Vegas posted the steepest decline (-1.9%), ahead of Seattle (-1.8%), Denver (-1.8%), and Tampa (-1.6%). On a monthly basis, home prices increased 0.9%, easing slightly from gains of 1.0% in April and 1.1% in March but continuing to reflect the seasonal strength typically seen during the spring homebuying season. On a seasonally adjusted basis, prices rose 0.2% from the previous month.

【世界首臺630攝氏度超超臨界煤電機組投運】中國大唐28日宣佈，大唐鄆城630攝氏度超超臨界二次再熱煤電機組當日全容量投運，這是世界首個再熱蒸汽溫度超過630攝氏度、發電熱效率突破50%的超超臨界煤電專案，單臺機組容量100萬千瓦。 中國大唐董事長呂軍表示，這一機組創造了同型別機組溫度最高、壓力最高、效率最高、供電煤耗最低的世界紀錄，進一步提高燃煤效率，降低度電耗煤量。 呂軍介紹，機組以技術革新、工藝升級、裝備迭代、材料創新為抓手，實現“新技術、新工藝、新材料、新裝備”突破與整合應用，裝置國產化率達98.89%，成功突破發電熱效率50%的行業技術“天花板”，在效率、溫度、壓力、煤耗等方面實現新突破。 這一專案是中國大唐聯合東方電氣、中國寶武、中國鋼研等全產業鏈聯合攻關取得的重大技術突破。採用多種新型耐高溫特種鋼材和自主研製C630R新型汽輪機轉子，同步整合應用變速小機帶功率平衡發電機雙機回熱系統、12級回熱抽汽系統等核心技術。 專案位於山東省鄆城縣，是“十三五”期間的國家重點專案，安裝2臺100萬千瓦超超臨界二次再熱燃煤發電機組，是我國攻克煤電關鍵核心技術的標誌性成果，為構建新型電力系統提供有力支撐。(新華社)

【王毅出席全球發展倡議5週年高階別會議】
2026年7月28日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在北京出席全球發展倡議5週年高階別會議並致辭。

王毅表示，5年前，面對全球發展事業何去何從的時代之問，習近平主席鄭重提出全球發展倡議，為重振全球發展事業提供了中國方案、作出了中國貢獻。5年來，倡議的理念引領效應持續釋放，各領域合作機制不斷完善，務實合作成果接連落地。當前國際形勢變亂交織，全球發展倡議的時代意義更加凸顯。我們要繼續高舉發展旗幟，堅定推進全球發展倡議與聯合國發展議程對接，共同創造美好未來。
一是加強團結合作，營造和平發展環境。中方將一如既往支援聯合國改革振興，強化發展支柱，增進廣大發展中國家福祉。

二是凝聚行動合力，加快落實2030年議程。中方將繼續向其他發展中國家提供更多的發展資源，推動解決發展不平衡不充分問題。

三是推進開放互鑑，壯大創新發展引擎。中方願同各方深化創新合作，助力全球南方充分把握新機遇、妥善應對新挑戰。

四是踐行多邊主義，完善全球治理體系。要加強政策協調，確保聯合國2030年後全球發展議程更好反映全球南方的普遍需求。

王毅強調，中國的發展離不開世界，世界的發展也離不開中國。無論國際形勢如何變化，中方將繼續發揮負責任大國作用，將自身發展同各國共同發展緊密聯絡起來，做全球發展的促進者和貢獻者，不斷推進落實全球發展倡議，同世界各國共享發展機遇，推動構建人類命運共同體。

本次會議由外交部、國家國際發展合作署、聯合國發展系統駐華機構聯合舉辦，主題為“全球發展倡議5週年再出發，構建可持續發展的美好未來”。聯合國副秘書長、秘書長辦公廳主任拉特雷，“全球發展倡議之友小組”成員國駐華使節，聯合國發展機構駐華代表等出席。各方高度評價5年來全球發展倡議取得的豐碩成果，讚賞中國在推進國際發展合作中發揮的引領作用，認為倡議完全契合聯合國憲章宗旨，有助於推動將發展置於全球議程的中心位置，為全球南方國家提供了寶貴機遇，願同中方持續深化發展合作，共同踐行多邊主義，攜手促進世界和平與繁榮。（外交部）

### 2026-07-28 20:44:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Goods Trade Balance #Importance 2

US Goods Trade Deficit Narrows in June — The US goods trade deficit narrowed to $101.5 billion in June 2026 from a 14-month high of $105.9 billion in May, according to preliminary data. Exports fell 1.8% to $204.7 billion, marking a second straight monthly decline and moving further below April's record high, driven by lower shipments of industrial supplies (-4.4%), foods, feeds, and beverages (-3.1%), and capital goods (-1.1%). Imports declined at a faster 2.6% to $306.2 billion, with purchases falling across all major categories, including consumer goods (-3.8%), capital goods (-2.0%), industrial supplies (-1.9%), vehicles (-2.5%), and foods, feeds, and beverages (-2.5%). In the first half of 2026, the cumulative goods trade deficit narrowed to $535.5 billion from $716.6 billion in the same period a year earlier. The improvement suggests trade flows are continuing to normalize following last year's US tariff announcements, although uncertainty surrounding US trade policy remains.

### 2026-07-28 20:42:32  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Apple #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #AAPL.O #Impact mixed

【Apple shifts from device purchase to monthly leasing — will it shorten upgrade cycles?】Apple has rolled out Apple Upgrade in the US, offering device leases via Klarna. Terms: iPhone and Apple Watch 12–24 months; Mac and iPad 24–36 months, matching typical replacement/use cycles. Leasing lowers the monthly payment barrier and embeds return, renewal or upgrade options at term, which could convert irregular upgrade demand into a more predictable update cadence. Lower monthly fees do not necessarily reduce total ownership cost; long-term holders may be no better off than with interest-free instalments. Whether the plan shortens upgrade cycles depends on adoption, end‑of‑term upgrade conversion and sustained renewal rates. Only repeated re‑enrolment would let Apple shift hardware from one‑off sales toward a steadier replacement-driven revenue pattern.

### 2026-07-28 20:41:14  #國際

【多瑙河布達佩斯段水位創歷史新低】匈牙利水資源管理總局28日釋出的資料顯示，多瑙河布達佩斯段水位已降至31釐米，創下該河段水位歷史新低。由於水位下降，多瑙河沿線多地相繼發現了二戰時期的彈藥和沉船等。 水利部門當天測得的資料打破了該河段2018年10月25日創下的33釐米最低紀錄。氣象與水利專家預測，未來幾天，多瑙河水位可能繼續下降。 鑑於罕見的高溫乾旱重新整理了水位紀錄，近日在多瑙河上的瑪格麗特島附近水域清理出一些二戰時期遺留炮彈。此外，據當地媒體報道，多瑙河沿線多地近期相繼發現了一些沉船殘骸。 近來持續高溫與降水不足使歐洲主要河流水位下降。國際科研機構“世界天氣歸因聯盟”23日釋出研究報告說，人類活動導致的氣候變化加劇了歐洲當前的嚴重乾旱。與降雨量不足相比，異常高溫是歐洲多地乾旱加重的更重要原因。(新華社)

### 2026-07-28 20:26:35  #其他

【河南“三支一扶”高分成績引爭議，官方再回應】據“河南人社”，河南省“三支一扶”領導小組協調辦公室7月28日再發宣告：近期，部分省轄市在公佈2026年省“三支一扶”人員招募面試資格確認人員名單時，有的省轄市包含筆試成績，有的省轄市僅公佈了人員名單、無筆試成績。為切實增強考試公開透明度，切實維護考試的公平公正，經河南省“三支一扶”工作領導小組協調辦公室研究決定，凡未公佈筆試成績的省轄市，統一於2026年7月30日15點之前，由各省轄市將進入面試資格確認人員名單及其筆試成績一併對外公佈。同時，針對網民質疑的考試嫌疑，省“三支一扶”領導小組協調辦公室正會同有關部門進行深入核查，查實一個取消一個，並依規依法處理。崗位空缺時，依考生報考崗位成績由高到低依次遞補進入面試資格人員。7月27日，河南省“三支一扶”工作領導小組協調辦公室釋出宣告：近日，網路平臺出現我省安陽、平頂山、駐馬店、濟源等地2026年高校畢業生“三支一扶”高分考生集中等相關輿情資訊，引發廣大考生、家長及社會各界關注。我們對此高度重視，第一時間成立工作組，針對網傳內容開展全面、嚴謹的調查核實工作，儘快查明真相，及時通報情況，如發現違規違紀問題，將移交有關部門嚴肅查處，切實保障廣大考生合法權益、維護考試公平公正。此前，據荔枝新聞報道，近日，河南“三支一扶”考試成績公佈，有網友在安陽市和濟源市的考生成績中發現規律，多個崗位出現高分斷層領先，第一名80多分，而第二名只有60多分。該網友指出“一人崗就有一個人考80分以上，兩人崗就有兩個人考80分以上”。

### 2026-07-28 20:21:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Mid-month Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Brazil Mid-Month Inflation Comes in Below Forecasts — Consumer prices in Brazil rose 0.06% in the month to mid-July 2026, slowing sharply from 0.41% in June and coming in below forecasts of 0.2%. The reading marked the lowest mid-month inflation rate since August 2025. Food and beverage prices provided the largest downward contribution, falling 0.66%. Food consumed at home dropped 1.14%, while food away from home accelerated to 0.55%. Housing was the main source of inflationary pressure, rising 0.97%, driven by a 3.03% increase in residential electricity prices following the introduction of the yellow tariff flag and regulated tariff adjustments in several cities. Transportation prices rose 0.11%, as an 11.7% jump in airfares more than offset lower fuel prices amid easing concerns over the Middle East oil supply crisis. Health and personal care costs increased 0.28%, led by higher personal hygiene product prices. The 12-month inflation rate eased to 4.52% from 4.58% in June.

【US media: Netanyahu's influence on Trump much reduced during this US visit】Axios reports Israeli Prime Minister Netanyahu will meet Trump in the Oval Office on Tuesday — their eighth meeting in Trump’s second term — but says Netanyahu’s sway over Trump is markedly weaker than on prior visits. The meeting could still matter as Trump weighs next steps, including possible coordinated action between the two countries. During the opening months after the Iran war began the men spoke almost daily to coordinate; Axios says call frequency has fallen sharply in recent weeks and Netanyahu now leans more on Secretary of State Rubio to read Trump’s thinking. Netanyahu reportedly struggled to secure the Tuesday slot after failing to visit last week. Since the April 8 ceasefire, Axios says Netanyahu’s influence on Trump’s Iran war policy has waned; Trump on Monday acknowledged differences with Netanyahu, and two weeks ago Netanyahu failed to convince Trump to drop an F-35 sale to Turkey.

### 2026-07-28 20:14:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

North Macedonia Holds Key Policy Rate at 4.25% — The National Bank of North Macedonia left its interest rate unchanged at 4.25% at its July 2026 meeting, citing uncertainty surrounding foreign prices and geopolitical tensions. Although headline inflation slowed in June to 3.4% from 4.8%, driven by lower food prices, policymakers stated more time was needing to assess price stability considering that inflation remains elevated, as foreign prices are still high and unstable due to geopolitical tensions and possible spill-over effects. Economic activity continues to show resilience, although growth slowed to 3.1% in the first quarter of 2026 from 3.8% in the prior quarter. Looking ahead, the NBNM stated it will continue to aim for stability of the denar against the euro, in order to ensur price stability in the medium term, and will closely monitor domestic and international economic and financial development.

【APEC forestry ministers adopt joint statement】The 2026 APEC forestry ministers meeting, held in Shenzhen on 27–28, adopted a joint statement calling for enhanced biodiversity protection and stronger enforcement against illegal trade in forest products. It urges economies to embed forest governance and sustainable forest management into economic development plans and policy frameworks. The statement asks members to expand finance and investment opportunities for forestry development and legal timber trade, tailored to national circumstances, and to support upgrading processing and value chains for forest products. It promotes deeper Asia‑Pacific cooperation on urban forestry and water‑resource management, and calls for using technological innovation and sustainable management to restore ecosystems and reduce habitat fragmentation. Under principles of sustainable forest management, it encourages development of emerging forest industries, products and services.

### 2026-07-28 19:45:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Current Account #Importance 1

Brazil Current Account Deficit Narrows More Than Expected — Brazil's current account deficit narrowed to $2.33 billion in June 2026 from $5.18 billion in the same month a year earlier, beating market expectations of a $2.45 billion shortfall. The improvement was largely driven by a stronger trade balance, with the surplus widening by $3.6 billion to $8.8 billion as goods exports surged 24.8% year-on-year to a record $36.4 billion, while imports rose 15.3% to $27.6 billion. Primary and secondary income balances were broadly unchanged from a year earlier, with the primary income deficit holding at $6.5 billion and the secondary income surplus edging up to $438 million. Meanwhile, the services deficit widened by $0.7 billion to $5.1 billion, reflecting higher net spending on international travel (21%), transportation (22.4%), and telecommunications, computing, and information services (16.9%).

### 2026-07-28 19:45:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Extend Mixed Momentum — Futures tracking US equity indices were mixed on Tuesday as chip producers extended their selloff, contrasting with relief on other sectors from lower Treasury yields. Contracts for the S&P 500 were flat, while those for the Nasdaq 100 dropped 1% and those for the Dow gained 350 points. Concerns that spending on AI infrastructure by hyperscalers is due to slow grappled compute and memory producers, with Micron, Sandisk dropping more than 7%. The sector was also punished by optimism on Chinese competitors following the strong CXMT IPO in Shanghai. Nvidia fell 1.5% and AMD lost around 5%. On the other hand, software-facing and non-tech shares were mostly higher. Credit costs extended their pullback, tracking lower energy pries as US officials signaled willingness for diplomacy with Iran. JPMorgan was higher to set the pace for the banks, while Visa gained 1% ahead of its earning release after the close. Also, Coca Cola gained 3% premarket after boosting its outlook.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Falls for 3rd Session — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell for a third consecutive session to 4.62% on Tuesday, its lowest level in about a week, as lower oil prices continued to support demand for government bonds. ### 2026-07-28 18:51:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Industrial Production #Importance 2

India Industrial Production Grows Most in 2 Years — Industrial production in India expanded by 7.3% from the previous year in June of 2026, firmly above market expectations of a 5.7% growth rate to mark the sharpest pace of expansion in nearly two years. The result consolidated the rebound from lower growth rates in the start of the second quarter, when the war in the Middle East triggered energy shortages and lifted input costs for power-intensive industries. Manufacturing production surged by 7.8%, the most since the start of the year. In the meantime, output continued to surge for electricity and gas supply (10.6% vs 10.3% in May) and rebounded for mining and quarrying (1.0% vs -1.4%). From the previous month, industrial output expanded by 0.5%.

市委副書記、市長、市委財經工作委員會副主任龔正，市委副書記朱忠明出席並講話。

會議指出，建設國際消費中心城市，是黨中央賦予上海的重要戰略任務，對於深化“五個中心”建設、發揮消費對經濟增長的基礎性作用、保障和改善民生，推動高質量發展、構建新發展格局意義重大。要準確把握消費變化趨勢，著眼供需兩端細分賽道，提高政策措施的針對性有效性，強化專業化、國際化的策劃、營運、組織、服務人才引育。要堅持系統觀念，在文商旅體展融合發展上深化拓展，推動多業態、多要素、多場景融合，持續用力、久久為功，打造一批更具影響力和標識性的消費大IP。要保持行動敏捷，搶抓用好智慧消費新機遇，大力拓展入境消費，加強特色商圈和特色街區建設，不斷髮現新需求、催生新模式、培育新業態，推動國際消費中心城市建設取得新突破、再上大臺階。

會議指出，未來產業培育事關搶佔科技和產業制高點、發展新質生產力，事關深化“五個中心”建設、提升城市核心競爭力，要緊緊圍繞產業生態構建，加快把未來產業培育成勢，在前沿和戰略必爭領域不斷取得新突破、贏得新優勢。要把握產業發展規律和技術底層邏輯，進一步深化工作機制建設，不斷調整最佳化對賽道佈局的動態研判機制，完善專案生成機制和專案經理團隊負責制，加快探索接力推進機制，健全概念驗證體系。市、區要加強協同，推動區裡錨定細分賽道、明確投入重點、提升專業能力。要抓牢高水平人才隊伍這個關鍵，聚焦重點方向，形成引才合力，為未來產業發展提供堅實支撐。（上海釋出）

Brent Falls for 3rd Session — Brent fell to around $86.5 per barrel on Tuesday, touching its lowest level in more than a week, as optimism over a potential diplomatic breakthrough between the US and Iran eased concerns about supply disruptions. President Donald Trump said Washington was engaged in “good talks” with Tehran and that there was a “good chance” of reaching a resolution after pausing military strikes to give negotiations more time. He cautioned, however, that US attacks would resume if diplomacy failed, while Iran also warned it would retaliate should talks collapse. Oman has reportedly proposed a joint regional mechanism to manage shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, including a voluntary fee system. Despite the improving diplomatic outlook, geopolitical risks remain elevated. Saudi Arabia said it intercepted drones launched from Iraq targeting petroleum facilities, blaming Iran-backed groups, while Yemen’s Houthi rebels claimed responsibility for an attack on Saudi Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline.

### 2026-07-28 18:19:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 1

Ireland Retail Activity Growth Holds Steady — Retail sales in Ireland rose by 0.4% month-on-month in June 2026, matching the pace recorded in the previous month. The largest volume increases were recorded in electrical goods (+1.8%), clothing, footwear, and textiles (+1.5%), bars (+1.2%), books, newspapers, and stationery (+1.2%). Fuel sales also strengthened, rising 6.0% in value and 0.4% in volume, reflecting elevated fuel prices amid ongoing geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Meanwhile, gains were partly capped by notable declines in furniture and lighting (-1.8%) and pharmaceuticals, medical, and cosmetic articles (-1.4%). On an annual basis, retail activity grew by 0.6% in June, easing from an upwardly revised 0.9% rise in the preceding period.

### 2026-07-28 18:12:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Unemployed Persons #Importance 1

French Unemployment Rises in June — France's registered unemployment rose by 5,900 to 3,121.5 thousand in June 2026, slowing from a 15,500 increase in May. The monthly increase was driven primarily by older job seekers, with the number of unemployed individuals aged 50 and over climbing by 5,400 to 779,800. Meanwhile, unemployment among those under 25 rose by 1,500 to 509,400, while the number of job seekers aged 25 to 49 declined by 1,000 to 1.812 million. By gender, registered unemployment among men increased by 7,400 to 1.622 million, more than offsetting a decline of 1,500 among women, whose total fell to 1.500 million.

### 2026-07-28 18:11:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Macedonia Producer Inflation Hits Over 1-Year High — The annual producer inflation rate in North Macedonia rose to 6.2% in June 2026 from a 6.0% increase in the previous month, marking the highest level since March 2025. Price growth continued for energy (10.9% vs 10.2% in May), intermediate goods excluding energy (4.7% vs 4.5%), and consumer goods (3.7% vs 3.0%), particularly non-durable goods (3.7% vs 2.9%). Meanwhile, prices eased for capital goods (10.6% vs 11.2%). By sectors and divisions, costs edged up in manufacturing (5.2% vs 5.1%), while inflation rose for mining and quarrying (24.5% vs 23.2%), electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (11.2% vs 10.7%), and water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation services (7.4% vs 6.4%). On a monthly basis, producer prices edged up 0.5% in June, down from 1.2% in May.

### 2026-07-28 18:09:15  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美元看漲邏輯穩固，基本面支撐超越美聯儲政策預期 】儘管當前美元的看漲押注已處於極端高位，但其背後的基本面支撐邏輯依然穩固，美元走強的驅動力已超越單純的美聯儲政策預期。根據美國商品期貨交易委員會（CFTC）的最新資料，美元多頭頭寸總量已攀升至近11年來的最高水平。理論上，極度擁擠的交易倉位往往容易引發反向擠壓，但當前市場結構表明，這更多反映了市場對美元長期看漲觀點的堅定共識。當前美元走強主要受兩大核心因素支撐。首先是地緣政治與能源市場的聯動。伊朗衝突的持續為美元提供了貿易條件改善與避險屬性的雙重支撐。儘管近期油價有所回落，但原油價格再次飆升的風險依然高企，這使得歐洲貨幣難以獲得實質性追捧，資金仍傾向於迴流美元資產。其次是人工智慧（AI）投資熱潮帶來的結構性利好。AI交易正在持續推動資金流入美國資產，同時提振了市場對美國經濟增長與通脹的預期。這不僅為美聯儲未來收緊貨幣政策提供了理由，擴大了美元的套利優勢，還在科技市場情緒惡化時，為美元提供了額外的避險需求。本週市場的最大不確定性在於美聯儲的利率決議。在彭博社調查的101位經濟學家中，僅有一位預計美聯儲將加息，這反映出市場對6月份通脹趨於平穩的共識，以及政策制定者傾向於等待美聯儲主席沃什（Kevin Warsh）領導的特別工作組取得進展。

【何立峰出席2026年亞太經合組織林業部長級會議開幕式並致辭】中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰28日在深圳出席2026年亞太經合組織林業部長級會議開幕式並致辭。 何立峰指出，進入新時代以來，在習近平生態文明思想科學指引下，中國生態文明建設從認識到實踐都發生了歷史性、轉折性、全域性性變化，在創造經濟快速發展、社會長期穩定這兩大奇蹟的同時，走出了一條人與自然和諧共生的中國式現代化道路。林業是生態文明建設的主要載體。中國始終把林業發展擺在突出位置，作出全面謀劃和系統部署，統籌推進高水平保護和高質量發展，取得了一系列重大成就。 何立峰強調，亞太是全球森林資源最富集、生態系統型別最多樣的地區之一，森林面積佔全球一半以上。中國始終致力於維護全球生態安全，傳播生態文化和綠色理念，為亞太地區乃至全球生態文明建設貢獻了中國智慧、中國方案。在各方共同努力下，亞太地區成為扭轉全球森林資源下降趨勢的主要力量。 何立峰指出，面對當前亞太地區生態形勢，中國將繼續辦好自己的事，深入踐行習近平生態文明思想，加快經濟社會發展全面綠色轉型。中國願同各方一道攜手並進、合作共贏，共同推動亞太地區林業高質量發展，共同書寫綠色亞太發展新篇章。(新華社)

【美銀：低收入群體消費增速反超富裕階層，美國經濟“K型分化”現收窄跡象】美國銀行（Bank of America）最新資料顯示，美國低收入家庭的信用卡支出增速目前已略微超過高收入群體，這標誌著近年來顯著的經濟“K型分化”消費趨勢正在發生逆轉。美銀在最新報告中指出，這一支出格局的轉變主要得益於三大核心因素的共同推動：一是勞動力市場表現強勁，帶動低收入群體的薪資收入實現增長；二是燃油等能源價格回落，直接減輕了底層消費者的生活成本壓力；三是有利的同比基數效應。長期以來，美國經濟呈現出典型的“K型”特徵，即高收入群體憑藉資產增值和薪資增長支撐了大部分消費支出，而中低收入群體則面臨通脹與債務的雙重擠壓。然而，最新資料表明，隨著底層勞動者薪資增速的加快以及生活成本的邊際改善，低收入群體的消費信心與購買力正得到實質性修復。

【MAS warns AI investment pullback could threaten global markets】MAS warned uncertainty from sustained, large-scale AI investment is a key risk to global growth and financial markets and flagged the longer-term threat of an escalation in the Middle East conflict. The MAS governor said the recent surge in spending on data centers, chips, compute infrastructure and semiconductor capacity has supported global growth, and that both a continued boom and a sharp withdrawal of funding would have material effects. A significant pullback could materially weaken global growth; a prolonged AI expansion would influence incomes, demand and inflation. MAS also flagged financial-stability risks from equity, credit and loan-market exposure to unsustainable business models within complex financing structures, which could impair cash flows and tighten credit conditions. It said global growth, investment and market performance are now highly dependent on expectations of continued large-scale data-center and semiconductor investment, notably in US capital markets and in Asian semiconductor-exporting economies.

【AI重塑全球客服行業：企業加速自動化程序引發崗位結構性調整】人工智慧（AI）正深刻重塑全球客戶服務行業生態。隨著微軟、優步（Uber）及澳大利亞聯邦銀行等企業加速引入AI自動化系統處理日常諮詢與呼叫中心業務，全球客服領域正經歷一輪顯著的崗位結構性調整。為提升運營效率並降低成本，多家跨國企業已將AI技術深度整合至客戶服務環節。據報道，微軟已將其客戶支援團隊規模從5萬人縮減至4萬人，標誌著其向AI驅動型服務模式轉型的決心。優步等企業也透過部署自動化助手與聊天機器人，接管了大量常規諮詢業務，從而最佳化響應時間並削減人力成本。儘管自動化浪潮導致部分重複性支援崗位的需求下降，但AI對就業市場的影響並非單純的“崗位消失”。隨著AI接管標準化、流程化的基礎工作，企業正逐步將人力釋放至更具價值的領域。

### 2026-07-28 17:42:18  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Meta Platforms #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #META.O #BLK.O

【Meta, BlackRock to jointly fund $14bn data-center campus in El Paso】Meta Platforms and BlackRock will jointly invest in and own a data-center campus under construction in El Paso, Texas, with 1 GW of compute capacity and estimated development costs of about $14 billion. BlackRock-managed funds will hold an 80% stake and Meta 20%; both parties will fund their proportional shares of campus construction and long-term power, cooling and communications infrastructure. Meta will provide construction, administrative and facilities management and will be the campus' initial sole user. The deal is expected to close within days and the campus is slated to begin operations in 2028; Meta says the El Paso project is part of more than $10 billion of its investments. Peak construction employment will exceed 4,000 with roughly 300 operations jobs; more than 2,300 workers are on site now.

### 2026-07-28 17:17:15  #MKT News #AMZN.O #Impact mixed

【Sources: Amazon shifts AI strategy, phases out multiple in-house models to focus on FMR】Business Insider, citing people familiar with the matter, reports Amazon (AMZN.O) is overhauling its AI strategy: winding down numerous internal text, image and video models, reorganizing teams, and concentrating engineers and limited compute on a single priority program called Frontier Model Research (FMR). The company is phasing out flagship models including Premier and Omni, the Reel video generator and Canvas image generator, and reallocating resources from its Nova model into FMR, led by researcher Pete Abbeel. Employees describe the retired models as in KTLO (keep-the-lights-on) status. Amazon is developing a new flagship base model under FMR, currently its top priority and expected to debut at this fall’s re:Invent conference.

### 2026-07-28 17:14:26  #宏觀 #公司

【兩部門調整部分能源資源行業企業城鎮土地使用稅政策】財政部、稅務總局7月28日對外發布公告，明確將調整部分能源資源行業企業城鎮土地使用稅政策。 根據此次釋出的公告，此前出臺的煤炭、礦山、油氣、建材、電力等行業企業的部分主要生產經營用地免徵城鎮土地使用稅優惠政策將取消。同時，上述企業中具有公益性、安全保障性質以及重大基礎設施用地的免稅政策將予以保留。 據瞭解，部分能源資源行業企業城鎮土地使用稅優惠政策已實施近40年。近年來，優惠政策長期實施帶來的土地利用效率偏低、影響稅收公平等負面效應日益突出。 “當初給予優惠政策時，能源資源行業的產品還在實行計劃定價，屬於需要支援的行業。經過近40年的發展，我國能源資源行業已今非昔比，這方面的優惠政策確實需要調整。”中國財科院公共收入研究中心主任梁季說。 她表示，上述行業土地使用面積大，繼續對其主要生產經營用地實施城鎮土地使用稅優惠政策，不利於企業節約集約使用土地。取消該優惠，可引導企業盤活存量土地，提高土地利用效率。 根據公告，政策調整設定了過渡期，分兩步到位。對恢復徵稅的土地，自2026年9月1日至2027年8月31日，按應納稅額減半徵收城鎮土地使用稅；自2027年9月1日起，全額徵收城鎮土地使用稅。(新華社)

---

### 2026-07-28 17:14:26  #公司

【TGV玻璃基板加速量產，玻璃基板相關企業5281家，多為資金密集型】據上海證券報訊息，眼下，國產TGV（玻璃通孔技術）玻璃基板正加速走向量產。國內中試產線跑通驗證，頭部企業紛紛加碼佈局，搶佔AI時代先進半導體封裝新機遇。作為下一代半導體先進封裝的核心底層技術，TGV玻璃通孔技術具備低損耗、高精密、高穩定性的核心優勢，可完美適配AI算力晶片、高階晶片的嚴苛封裝需求，是當前半導體新材料迭代升級的核心主流方向。伴隨全球AI算力產業高速爆發，高階晶片對封裝基材的傳輸效率、精度與穩定性要求持續升級，TGV技術產業化價值凸顯，市場成長空間廣闊。企查查資料顯示，截至7月28日，國內現存玻璃基板相關企業5281家。從經營時間來看，相關企業多以成熟企業為主，成立年限在10年以上企業佔比60.55%。從註冊資本分佈來看，相關企業多為資金密集型企業，註冊資本在5000萬元及以上企業佔比41.28%。從地區分佈來看，玻璃基板相關企業具備較強的地域集中性。分佈在華東地區的企業存量佔比44.14%，華南地區佔29.99%，兩大區域合計超74%。

【《詞元雲服務行業自律公約》正式釋出：規範詞元雲服務體系，賦能可信雲智未來】為凝聚行業發展共識，順應技術與服務產業發展規律，明晰行業主體行為邊界，統一服務等級協議規範，保障服務供給方、使用者及相關各方合法權益，防範各類業務與合規風險，夯實行業長效健康發展根基，由中國資訊通訊研究院（簡稱“中國信通院”）牽頭倡議，聯合詞元雲服務領域相關從業企業、機構共同起草釋出了《詞元雲服務行業自律公約》（以下簡稱《公約》）。2026年7月28日，《公約》釋出儀式在2026可信雲大會暨首屆Token雲服務大會主論壇上舉行。中國信通院、天翼雲、移動雲、聯通數科、阿里雲、華為雲、火山引擎、騰訊雲、百度、京東、金山雲、MiniMax、階躍星辰、聯想、清程極智等多家企業代表共同見證了《公約》的正式釋出。《公約》秉承合法合規、使用者至上、公平競爭、開放協作、安全為本五大核心原則。發起方共同致力於構建“安全可靠、誠信可信、良性發展、規範有序”的詞元雲服務生態。透過築牢技術與合規雙重防線，保障使用者資料與資產安全；恪守商業道德與職業操守，構建穩定的信任協作關係；摒棄不正當競爭，讓優質服務與技術創新成為核心競爭力；以公約為準繩，推動行業標準化建設，形成權責清晰、運轉高效的良性發展格局。（中國信通院）

【中國代表就烏克蘭問題發聲：全力推動局勢降溫】中國常駐聯合國代表團臨時代辦孫磊27日在安理會烏克蘭問題緊急公開會上發言，敦促停止任何針對平民的襲擊，呼籲當事方保持冷靜剋制，嚴格遵守國際人道法，保護平民和民用基礎設施安全，全力推動局勢降溫，為早日實現和平爭取希望。孫磊說，當前烏克蘭問題局勢發展令人擔憂，衝突烈度持續升級。當務之急是實現停火止戰。戰事升級只會給民眾帶來更沉重的苦難。早日停火止戰符合當事方根本和長遠利益，也順應國際社會共同期待。孫磊指出，根本之道是堅持對話談判。當前主要各方立場存在很大分歧，談判之路艱難曲折。中方呼籲有關各方保持接觸，展現政治意願，在對話中增進互信，在談判中積累共識，爭取達成全面、持久、有約束力的和平協議。（新華社）

### 2026-07-28 16:59:45  #公司 #市場

【麥格理：大幅下調老鋪黃金目標價至275港元，維持“跑輸大市”評級】麥格理髮表報告指，老鋪黃金上半年營收同比增60-66%，較該行預期低17-19%；non-IFRS經調整淨利同比增83-85%，較該行預期低27%。推算次季營收約為23億至39.5億元，淨利僅5.1億至7.6億元。該行認為，由於高基數、金價不穩、競爭加劇及炒家去庫存，下半年同店銷售增長仍可能面臨下行風險。該行維持對其“跑輸大市”評級，因次季業績下調同店銷售及利潤率預測，目標價下調39%至275港元。該行預期毛利率仍有下行空間，因低成本黃金原材料可能已耗盡，且在需求疲弱下，黃金珠寶市場促銷或更為激烈。公司擬聚焦新品釋出及高價值客戶管理，可能推高營運開支，進而拖累營業利潤。該行將2026至28年淨利預測分別下調39%至41%，以反映上半年業績不及預期，以及對同店銷售與利潤率更為保守的假設。

【澤連斯基赴美會晤特朗普：聚焦防空合作與和談前景，美烏關係顯著回暖】烏克蘭總統弗拉基米爾·澤連斯基（Volodymyr Zelenskiy）於當地時間7月28日抵達美國華盛頓，將與美國總統唐納德·特朗普（Donald Trump）舉行會晤。此次訪問旨在鞏固兩國近期回暖的雙邊關係，並尋求美方在防空能力上的進一步支援，以抵禦俄羅斯日益加劇的導彈襲擊。根據白宮日程安排，兩國領導人將於華盛頓時間上午9:30在橢圓形辦公室舉行會談。澤連斯基在社交媒體上明確表示，反導防禦及戰略合作是此次會晤的首要議題，並強調烏方致力於進一步促進和平16。此外，澤連斯基還計劃出席已故共和黨參議員林賽·格雷厄姆（Lindsey Graham）的葬禮。作為烏克蘭的堅定支持者，格雷厄姆生前曾十次訪問烏克蘭，並大力推動對俄製裁法案。在防空援助方面，澤連斯基正尋求獲得更多“愛國者”防空攔截導彈，以保護基輔及其他城市免受俄軍空襲。特朗普此前曾在7月初的北約峰會上表示，將授權烏克蘭生產“愛國者”導彈，但分析指出，建立生產線需要數年時間，無法解決烏克蘭當前面臨的彈藥短缺危機。因此，此次會晤中關於短期彈藥補給及長期聯合生產細節的討論，將成為雙方博弈的焦點。

### 2026-07-28 16:52:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Rubber Remains Range-Bound — Rubber futures traded around 218 US cents per kilogram in late July, continuing to move within a tight range since early this month amid the absence of a clear market direction. Trading remained subdued as lower oil prices following a pause in hostilities between the US and Iran reduced the cost advantage of natural rubber over synthetic alternatives. Demand concerns also persisted as the EU's anti-dumping duties on Chinese car and light truck tires continued to weigh on the outlook by raising the risk of weaker Chinese tire exports and lower consumption. Meanwhile, ongoing supply constraints in Southeast Asia due to El Niño continued to provide underlying support, offsetting some of the downward pressure on prices. Elsewhere, Thailand's natural rubber exports, excluding compound rubber, totaled 1.203 million tonnes in the first half of 2026, down 13% from a year earlier.

### 2026-07-28 16:48:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Slovenia Retail Sales Hit 4-Month Low — Slovenia's retail sales rose 2.1% year-on-year in June 2026, easing slightly from an upwardly revised 2.2% increase in the previous month and marking the weakest growth since February. The slowdown was driven by a 1.8% increase in retail sale of automotive fuel in specialised stores. Meanwhile, food climbed 1.6, while non-food retail sales grew 1.5%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales fell 0.2% in June, reversing an upwardly revised 0.1% gain in May. In the first half of 2026, retail turnover was 1.9% from the same period a year earlier, supported by a 6.4% jump in motor fuel sales and a 2.5% rise in non-food retail sales. By contrast, food retail sales declined 0.9%, underscoring continued softness in consumer demand for food products.

### 2026-07-28 16:46:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Slips from One-Year High — Lumber prices fell below $650 per thousand board feet after reaching a near one-year high, as new U.S. tariffs on most Canadian wood-related products weighed on the market. Although Canadian softwood lumber was exempt from Washington's new 50% tariffs, 98 other wood-product categories remained subject to the duties, deflating hopes of a lumber carve-out. Meanwhile, the Canadian government announced additional financial support for lumber producers in Western Canada to help offset tariffs and trade uncertainty. The Forest Products Association of Canada described the measures as a direct blow to the country's forestry sector, where mills are already operating below capacity, as Canadian duties of nearly 35% on lumber forced production cuts and mill closures. Still, wildfires across Western Canada continued to threaten lumber availability, while President Donald Trump warned that wildfire-related costs could be incorporated into existing duties.

【北京出臺街區控規改革實施意見 推動城市更新從“好房子”向“好街區”躍升】近日，北京市規劃和自然資源委員會印發《北京市深化與城市更新相適應的街區控規管理改革實施意見》（以下簡稱《實施意見》）。《實施意見》堅持以規劃街區為基本單元整體謀劃，創新建立分層編制、分級審批、全程監管的街區控規編管體系，在堅持空間剛性傳導基礎上，從時間維度為存量地區更新改造留足彈性，將城市更新實施方案、城中村改造方案作為規劃綜合實施方案的特定型別，與規劃綜合實施方案“合二為一”；按照規劃引領生成專案的工作思路，推廣“四步走、八清單”規劃方法，從空間規劃轉向謀劃策劃，引導資源在時間上精準投放；推動存量地區的實施重點轉向公共環境、公共空間、公共服務，鼓勵探索功能複合利用、規模流量管理、市政交通向好式改善等適應城市更新的規劃標準規範；引領市區部門統籌政策資金，以公共投入帶動價值提升進而帶動市場積極參與，以政府有為和市場有效共同推動“好房子”向“好街區”整體躍升。

【美股科技股與韓國股市關聯度升至2021年來最高水平，分析師警示風險】隨著人工智慧（AI）支出將美國科技巨頭和韓國儲存晶片製造商的命運聯絡在一起，美國科技股和韓國股市之間的關聯度正變得越來高。根據資管公司Rayliant提供的資料，韓國綜合KOSPI和納斯達克100指數之間的60天相關性最近攀升至0.50左右，至2021年以來的最高水平。這種日益增長的關係反映了三星電子和SK海力士的主導地位日益增強，這兩家公司加起來佔韓國綜合KOSPI股指權重的一半以上。這兩家公司都處於AI硬體供應鏈的中心，為美國科技巨頭運營的資料中心提供所需的儲存晶片。Futurum Group分析師巴爾克（Rolf Bulk）稱，“由於KOSPI股指基本上已成為一種半導體指數，其與美股科技股的相關性大幅增加。這種關聯性增加也伴隨著風險。”（第一財經）

14、特朗普批評《紐約時報》，並稱將繼續對《紐約時報》追究責任。

FTSE 100 Edges Up, Unilever Surges — The FTSE 100 traded modestly higher on Tuesday, with strong gains in consumer stocks offsetting weakness in banks and energy shares. Unilever surged more than 6% after the consumer goods group reported better-than-expected results, driven by robust performance in the US and emerging markets, while also raising its outlook for 2026. Barclays was among the biggest laggards, falling more than 5% despite reporting a 16% increase in second-quarter group income. The lender benefited from strong investment banking and US consumer operations amid heightened market volatility, but the results prompted profit-taking and weighed on the broader banking sector. Energy stocks also remained under pressure as oil prices extended recent losses. Shell slipped 0.8% and BP eased 0.4% after US President Donald Trump said there was a good chance of reaching an agreement with Iran, raising expectations of easing geopolitical tensions and reducing concerns over potential disruptions to global crude supplies.

【普徠仕：美聯儲會議若出現超過三張反對票，可能預示著將轉向9月加息】普徠仕表示，本週美聯儲會議上的反對票數量將受到密切關注，以此作為衡量聯邦公開市場委員會（FOMC）內部觀點分歧程度的指標。該機構美國首席經濟學家Blerina Uruci在一份報告中表示，預計投票結果將為10票贊成、2票反對，其中洛根和哈瑪克將投反對票，主張加息25個基點。Uruci指出，如果反對票超過三張，即便7月維持利率不變，這也將是一個更明確的訊號，表明委員會正朝著9月加息的方向邁進。該經濟學家的基準預測是，未來12個月內FOMC將維持利率不變。她還表示：“我認為，沃什對於9月加息的意願，並不像當前市場定價所暗示的那樣強烈。”

【量化巨頭盯上量子計算，今年已註冊相關企業1.4萬家，近三成在華東】量化巨頭盯上了量子計算。據證券時報，近日，成立僅三個月的量子計算作業系統及演算法平臺公司北京中科量樞科技有限公司官宣，已完成天使及天使+輪融資。據透露，兩輪融資金額均約數千萬元。融資將主要用於產品研發和團隊組建，其中，天使輪融資由普華資本、知壹投資聯合領投，九坤創投跟投；天使+輪由北工量子基金獨家投資。值得注意的是，這不是九坤創投第一次投向量子計算賽道。據媒體報道，九坤創投此前還曾跟投北京量子計算企業玻色量子科技股份有限公司的Pre-A輪融資。企查查資料顯示，截至7月28日，國內現存量子計算相關企業10.66萬家。從地區分佈來看，華南地區、華東地區分別以佔比25.88%、24.58%，位居前二；從註冊資本分佈來看，註冊資本在100萬元以內的企業存量最多，佔比48.58%。註冊量方面，2021年以來相關企業每年註冊量均在2萬家以上，截至目前今年已註冊1.4萬家相關企業，今年以來新註冊企業主要分佈在華東、華南地區。專利方面，截至目前，國內量子計算相關專利量達5386項，2025年相關專利申請量為939項。

FTSE MIB Edges Higher — The FTSE MIB edged higher to around 52,100 on Tuesday, modestly extending gains from the previous session as sentiment continued to improve following easing oil prices amid a pause in hostilities between the US and Iran. Investors also continued to digest a fresh batch of corporate earnings. Lottomatica gained more than 3% after reporting better-than-expected earnings and reaffirming its upbeat 2026 outlook. Other top performers included Ferrari (+1.5%), Leonardo (+1.9%), Mediobanca (+1.1%), and Banca Monte dei Paschi di Siena (+1.1%). Meanwhile, Saipem plunged more than 7% after the oil and gas contractor lowered its 2026 core earnings guidance to reflect additional costs related to the Middle East crisis and reported a sharp drop in second-quarter profit. Other notable laggards included technology-related stocks, with STMicroelectronics and Prysmian falling more than 1% and 2%, respectively, tracking the global technology selloff.

### 2026-07-28 16:01:28  #宏觀 #公司

【財政部：關於調整部分能源資源行業企業城鎮土地使用稅政策的公告】現就調整部分能源資源行業企業城鎮土地使用稅政策有關事項公告如下： 一、對按《國家稅務局關於電力行業徵免土地使用稅問題的規定》〔（89）國稅地字第013號〕等檔案（具體見附件）規定減免城鎮土地使用稅的土地，自2026年9月1日至2027年8月31日，按應納稅額減半徵收城鎮土地使用稅；自2027年9月1日起，全額徵收城鎮土地使用稅，（89）國稅地字第013號等檔案同時廢止。 二、自2026年9月1日起，對企業自有或透過承租、無償使用等方式取得並用於下列用途的土地，免徵城鎮土地使用稅： （一）石油天然氣（含頁岩氣、煤層氣）生產企業在工礦區內的消防、防洪排澇、防風、防沙設施用地，廠區（含生產、辦公、生活區）以外的鐵路專用線、公路用地； （二）油氣長輸管道線路用地及施工臨時用地； （三）水電站的水庫水面淹沒區、大壩（不包括發電廠房用地）、堤防、護坡、航道（含航道設施）、增殖放流站、過魚設施、灌溉設施和洩洪設施用地； （四）核電站的堤防、護坡用地； （五）火電廠廠區（含生產、辦公、生活區）以外的鐵路專用線、公路用地； （六）供電企業的輸電線路（含杆塔基礎）用地及其施工臨時用地； （七）煤炭企業的炸藥庫庫房外安全區用地，廠區（含生產、辦公、生活區）以外的鐵路專用線、公路、向社會開放的公園和公共綠化帶用地； （八）礦山企業（含金屬礦產和非金屬礦產）、建材企業的炸藥庫庫房外安全區用地。 企業的上述用地在專案建設期間可按本條規定免徵城鎮土地使用稅。 三、納稅人享受本公告規定的減免稅政策，應按規定進行減免稅申報，並將不動產權屬證明、臨時用地批覆、專案立項規劃檔案、土地用途證明等資料留存備查。

【2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會媒體圓桌會在北京召開】7月27日，2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會媒體圓桌會在北京召開。工業和資訊化部裝備工業一司司長郭守剛，交通運輸部科技司副司長翁優靈，北京市經濟和資訊化局副局長蘇國斌，工業和資訊化部裝備工業發展中心主任、世界智慧網聯汽車大會組委會秘書長瞿國春，中國汽車工業協會副會長兼秘書長付炳鋒，中國汽車工程學會副秘書長公維潔，中汽中心首席專家、中國汽車標準化研究院總工程師孫航，中國資訊通訊研究院技術與標準研究所車聯網與智慧交通研究部主任葛雨明等專家代表出席會議。會議由工業和資訊化部新聞發言人、辦公廳副主任龔憲主持，十餘家中央及行業主流媒體記者參加。付炳鋒介紹智慧網聯汽車發展情況與未來趨勢。他表示，當前全球智慧網聯汽車產業正處於從組合駕駛輔助向自動駕駛跨越的關鍵視窗期，呈現出“競爭激烈、多元探索、生態重構”的產業格局。聯合國及世界主要國家紛紛制定發展戰略、完善法規政策，支援自動駕駛技術加快研發應用。我國充分發揮政策引領、技術創新、跨界協同、市場驅動等方面的優勢，智慧網聯汽車產業整體發展水平處於國際第一梯隊。在重點技術進展方面，中國企業在“端到端”自動駕駛大模型應用上積極追趕，在“車路雲一體化”方面走在世界前列，在人機互動、多模態感知等方面實現全球領先，在鐳射雷達、大算力晶片、通訊終端等核心部件方面形成“量產降本、反哺技術”的良性迴圈，推動構建產業鏈優勢。未來，智慧網聯汽車在技術路線、產品形態、科技生態、跨界融合等方面將持續深化變革，世界智慧網聯汽車大會是連線技術創新、產業實踐與國際合作的重要平臺，對推動全球汽車產業轉型升級和建設更加安全、便捷、可持續的出行體系具有重要意義。（中國信通院）

### 2026-07-28 15:59:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

South Africa Leading Index Falls 0.3% MoM — The composite leading business cycle indicator in South Africa fell by 0.3% month-over-month in May 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 2.0% decline in the previous month. This marked the second consecutive monthly contraction, with weakness in five of the ten available component time series, while the other five provided support. The largest negative contributors included weaker business confidence, fewer building plans approved for flats, townhouses and larger houses, and a decline in the commodity price index for South Africa’s main export products. Additional pressure came from shorter average hours worked per factory worker in manufacturing and a narrower interest rate spread between 10-year government bonds and 91-day Treasury bills. Meanwhile, support came from gains in real M1 money supply growth, new passenger vehicle sales, domestic manufacturing orders, job advertisements, and the composite leading business cycle indicator for South Africa’s major trading partners.

Pound Falls to One-Month Low as Fed Outlook Boosts Dollar — The British pound fell below $1.33, its weakest level in nearly a month, as investors tracked developments in the Middle East while the US dollar gained support from lingering expectations that the Federal Reserve could still raise interest rates on Wednesday. Although a pause in US attacks on Iran pushed oil prices lower and eased inflation concerns, US Treasury yields declined only modestly, reflecting persistent market caution. Investors are also awaiting the Bank of England's policy decision later this week, with markets widely expecting interest rates to remain unchanged at 3.75%. Recent inflation data has reinforced that outlook, with annual consumer price growth slowing to a 15-month low of 2.6% in June, below the BoE's projections. A delayed pass-through of higher wholesale energy prices to regulated household bills has kept UK inflation below levels seen in the US and the euro area, where central banks are still expected to raise interest rates in September or October.

【商務部：歐盟炒作所謂人民幣匯率問題，不利於中歐經貿關係穩定發展】7月28日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行介紹所謂“產能過剩”問題中方立場有關情況的新聞釋出會。有媒體記者現場提問：近期一些來自歐盟國家及機構的領導人對中國貨幣被低估、為中國出口商帶來不公平優勢的問題表示了擔憂。對此，商務部的回應是什麼？展望今年下半年，尤其是在當前貿易緊張局勢持續以及整體經濟大背景下，商務部將如何看待中歐貿易前景？商務部政策研究室主任林衛龍在會上作出了回應。近年來，在中歐雙方領導人戰略引領下，中歐經貿關係總體發展良好。今年上半年，中國與歐盟貨物貿易總額同比增長14.2%，達到4478.8億美元。雙方在服務貿易、科技創新、綠色經濟等領域合作潛力巨大。這既是中歐產供鏈深度互嵌、優勢互補的結果，也是全球數字、綠色轉型和中歐未來發展的大勢所趨。與此同時，歐盟近期不斷推出對華貿易限制措施，並炒作所謂人民幣匯率問題，這缺乏事實依據，不利於中歐經貿關係的穩定發展。中國堅持市場在匯率形成中發揮決定性作用，保持人民幣匯率在合理均衡水平上的基本穩定。中國是負責任大國，不會透過匯率貶值獲取競爭優勢；中國製造具有國際競爭力，沒有必要透過匯率低估來促進出口。（澎湃）

### 2026-07-28 15:47:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Spain Jobless Rate at 2008-Lows — The unemployment rate in Spain fell to 9.87% in the second quarter of 2026, the lowest since the first quarter of 2008, compared to 10.83% in the previous period and forecasts of 10.7%. The number of unemployed persons declined by 213,300 to 2.495 million, with unemployment falling in all sectors: services (-170,300), agriculture (-22,400), industry (-10,900) and construction (-5,400). Unemployment fell by 57,700 among people who had been out of work for more than a year, while it increased by 53,400 among first-time job seekers. By gender, the number of unemployed men fell by 67,900 while the number of unemployed women declined by 145,400. The female unemployment rate dropped to 11.02%, while the male unemployment rate fell to 8.84%. Meanwhile, employment increased by 486,00 to 22.779 million, rising in services (+394,000), construction (+60,000) and industry (+38,200) while falling in agriculture (-6,200).

【王京濤出席第五屆中國IPv6創新發展大會】7月28日，第五屆中國IPv6創新發展大會在雄安新區舉辦。中央網信辦副主任、國家網信辦副主任王京濤出席大會開幕式並致辭。王京濤在致辭中表示，黨中央、國務院高度重視下一代網際網路發展，五年來，中央網信辦會同有關部門加強統籌協調、政策支援、系統推進和督促落實，各地區各部門認真貫徹中央網信委決策部署，推動我國IPv6規模部署和應用實現跨越式發展，截至2025年底，我國IPv6活躍使用者數達8.69億，五年增長88%，行動網路IPv6流量佔比達70.85%，固定網路IPv6流量佔比達32.38%，五年分別增長3.1倍和7.1倍，“十四五”8項主要指標均超額完成目標。“十五五”時期是IPv6從規模部署向全面賦能跨越的關鍵時期，要強化協調聯動，紮實推進《深化網際網路協議第六版（IPv6）技術創新和融合應用實施方案（2026—2030年）》落地見效；深化升級改造，鞏固提升IPv6發展成果；拓展創新空間，打造智慧網際網路關鍵底座；強化融合應用，推動IPv6賦能各行業數智化轉型；築牢安全屏障，打造內生安全的IPv6防護體系。第五屆中國IPv6創新發展大會由推進IPv6規模部署和應用專家委員會主辦，大會發布了《中國IPv6發展報告（2026）》，舉行人工智慧大模型IPv6能力提升專項行動啟動活動、雄安IPv6單棧部署行動啟動儀式，來自政府、高校、科研機構、企業、行業協會等500餘名代表參加了大會。

Euro Drops to One-Month Low as Fed Rate Outlook Supports Dollar — The euro slipped below $1.14, touching its weakest point in a month, as investors continued to monitor developments in the Middle East while the dollar found support from lingering expectations that the Federal Reserve could still raise interest rates on Wednesday. Although a pause in US attacks on Iran pushed oil prices lower and eased inflation concerns, US Treasury yields retreated only modestly, reflecting continued caution. In Europe, money markets slightly pared expectations for additional ECB tightening but still price in nearly two 25 bp rate hikes by March 2027. ECB policymaker Peter Kazimir said at least one more rate increase will likely be needed to curb inflation, adding that a weaker economic outlook could justify even more tightening than markets currently anticipate, while Chief Economist Philip Lane said the current inflation shock remains moderate, supporting further policy tightening. Investors now await fresh inflation data later this week for further policy clues.

【市場監管基層基礎能力建設專題研討會在京召開】7月26日至7月27日上午，市場監管基層基礎能力建設專題研討會在京召開。會議以習近平新時代中國特色社會主義思想為指導，深入學習領會習近平黨建思想、習近平總書記關於樹立和踐行正確政績觀的重要論述，全面貫徹黨的二十大和二十屆歷次全會精神，認真落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，以正確政績觀為引領，推動實施《市場綜合監管體系建設“十五五”規劃》，大力推進市場監管基層基礎能力建設。總局黨組書記、局長羅文出席會議並講話。

會議指出，近年來，在各級黨委、政府大力支援下，全系統持續加強基層基礎能力建設，因地制宜最佳化力量配置、強化資源保障、提升能力素養，進行了一系列改革探索，取得了積極成效。基層履職條件得到改善，隊伍專業化建設持續推進，資金投入渠道不斷拓寬，科技賦能取得積極成效，系統行風狀況向好轉變。

會議強調，要清醒認識基層基礎能力建設存在的突出問題，牢固樹立大抓基層、服務基層的鮮明導向，紮實推進基層基礎能力建設重點任務，加快健全末端發力、終端見效的監管機制。一是最佳化各層級職能配置，健全上下聯動工作機制，推動監管職責與資源能力相匹配。二是加大市場監管所建設力度，以等級評定為抓手，推動加強資源配備、完善制度機制、提高監管能力，切實為基層增效減負。三是增強技術機構專業能力，著力最佳化機構佈局、強化公益屬性、更新儀器裝置、填補建設空白。四是深入開展執法隊伍“清風鐵紀”教育整頓，聚焦“為何執法、為誰執法”突出抓好思想作風建設，嚴肅查處違規違紀行為，常態長效開展風紀監督。五是大力提升監管執法隊伍專業水平，推動最佳化隊伍結構，提高隊伍業務能力，加強專業隊伍建設。

會議強調，今年下半年是市場監管“十五五”規劃啟動實施的關鍵階段，要全面貫徹黨中央、國務院決策部署，牢固樹立和踐行正確政績觀，堅持以能力建設為主線、以基層基礎為重點、以提高監管效能為目標，加快完善與超大規模市場健康發展相適應的市場綜合監管體系，為推動經濟社會高質量發展貢獻更大力量。

【MOFCOM: China's industrial capacity utilization overall within a reasonable range】At a July 28 State Council Information Office briefing, Lin Weilong, director of MOFCOM’s Policy Research Office, said China’s industrial capacity utilization is broadly within a reasonable range. Capacity utilization at industrial enterprises above designated size averaged 74.4% in 2025; utilization is stronger in high‑tech manufacturing, high‑end equipment and strategic emerging industries, while some traditional raw‑material sectors show temporary underuse linked to structural adjustment and the green transition—a normal phase of industrial upgrading. Overall, China’s industrial supply and demand are basically balanced and operating stably.

【Global chip sell-off intensifies; U.S. tech stocks may slide】Global semiconductor shares are being sold off and worries that rising competition from Chinese firms could curb AI investment have increased, likely weighing on U.S. technology stocks. ING chief investment strategist Vincent Juvyns said trimming semiconductor positions is reasonable—a chance to take profits and diversify—but still recommends maintaining exposure given a clear multi-year earnings outlook, calling the rout a normal correction in a long-term growth cycle. CITIGROUP data show Nasdaq-100 long positions remain underwater, raising the risk of further pullbacks.

### 2026-07-28 15:25:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Falls from Over 1-Month High — Nickel fell to around $17,030 per tonne in late July, retreating from an over one-month high near $17,300, as investors locked in profits following a strong rally in recent weeks. Prices also came under pressure after Indonesia moved to resolve regulatory bottlenecks that had delayed exports of some nickel products, easing concerns over near-term supply disruptions. The move followed reports that the government is working to address uncertainty over the allowable rare-earth content in mineral shipments, which had disrupted exports of nickel products and alumina. Still, losses were limited by signs of tighter market conditions, with LME nickel inventories declining for six consecutive sessions, while support from Indonesian mining policies and slower nickel ore shipments from the Philippines had helped lift prices.
